# Unchecked memory accesses in library/Plank/Plank (inventory for the checked-access build)

Library: Plank master 3a6a28a (Plank-Lab pin). Generated by `fuzz/tools/checked-inventory.py` from the
checked-access rewriter's `sites.tsv` (Plank.Fuzzing.Checked/README.md) plus this hand-written review. Regenerate:
build with `-p:PlankCheckedAccess=true`, then
`python3 fuzz/tools/checked-inventory.py library/Plank/Plank/obj/Release/net10.0/checked-access/sites.tsv fuzz/notes/unchecked-access-sites.header.md fuzz/notes/unchecked-access-sites.md`.

Scope: every `Unsafe.*`, `MemoryMarshal.*`, `Vector*.LoadUnsafe/StoreUnsafe`, X86 pointer loads/stores and gathers,
pointer dereference/indexing, `fixed`, `new Span<T>(void*, int)`, `ref span[i]`, `Buffer.MemoryCopy` in the 224
library files and in Plank.InternalGen's generated `DeltaBinaryPackedDecoder.FastInteger.g.cs` (bit-unpacking
miniblocks, 400 KB). Span indexing, slicing, `BinaryPrimitives` on spans, `MemoryMarshal.Cast/AsBytes/Read` are
bounds-checked by the runtime and only listed as "safe".

"Bound applied" is what the checked build enforces at run time. The *logical* span is the span the ref was taken
from (`GetReference(payload)` -> the page payload slice the caller passed, not the buffer behind it), so a read past
`payload.Length` throws even if the bytes behind it belong to the same pooled allocation.

## Not checked, reviewed by hand

| Site | What | Review |
|---|---|---|
| `ParquetBuffer.cs` 29/49/119/128/133 | `Unsafe.AsRef<T>(_data)` / `new Span<byte>(_data, _length)`: the roots of every native buffer | hand rewrite: each view registers `[_data, _data + _length)`; the projected spans are checked against it (`AsSpan<T>`/`AsReadOnlySpan<T>` validate `count`, `AsValidatedSpan<T>` relies on its callers: now checked) |
| `ParquetBuffer.cs`, `DefaultParquetBufferPool.cs` `header->...`, `*(int*)allocation` | pool bookkeeping in front of the payload | allocator metadata, not a logical span (guard pages cover the allocation) |
| `Compression/ZlibNative.cs` `state->...` | zlib `z_stream` fields | native struct, fixed size |
| `Reading/GzipInflater.cs` 32-33, `Writing/Compression/GzipDeflater.cs` 43-47 | zlib `SetInput/SetOutput(ptr, len)` | lengths are `input.Length - consumed` / `outputBuffer.Length` of the pinned spans: correct |
| `Reading/ParquetDecompressor.cs` 95, `Writing/Compression/ZstdCompression.cs` 16 | `ZSTD_decompress/compress(out, outLen, in, inLen)` | lengths are the pinned spans' lengths: correct |
| `Reading/Logical/Internal/ColumnChunkReader.cs` 4117-4180 | `GatherDictionaryInt64*(…, long* dictionaryPointer, …)` | pointer passed from a registered `fixed`; the gathers/derefs inside are checked against it |
| `Reading/Logical/Internal/BinaryValueDescriptor.cs` 33 | `new ReadOnlySpan<byte>((void*)(payloadAddress + offset), length)` | hand rewrite: checked against the payload area registered by `GetBinaryPayloadAddress` / `ColumnBuffer` |
| `RowApi/RowReaderCore.cs` 252 | `ref Unsafe.Add(ref Unsafe.AsRef<T>((void*)batch.Address), _currentBatchOffset)` (row-API fast path, no bound) | hand rewrite: the batch carries `values.Length`; offset checked |
| `RowApi/RowApiColumnReadStateOfT.cs` 78, `Writing/Encoding/ByteStreamSplitEncoding.cs` 747 | `Unsafe.AsPointer(ref …)` | address only; the store through it (`Sse2.StoreScalar`) is checked against the hint of the `Unsafe.Add` it came from |
| `Reading/Logical/Internal/DeltaBinaryPackedDecoder.cs` 802/840/1360, `Writing/Encoding/ByteArrayRow.cs` 81/114, `Writing/Encoding/Encoding.cs` 3821 | `Unsafe.AsRef(in x)` (readonly -> mutable ref of an element/struct) | same address as an element of a registered span; walks from it are checked as derived refs when the element ref was registered, else unknown |
| `RowApi/RowReaderBinaryValue.cs` 31 | `Unsafe.IsNullRef` | no access |

## Outside this build

- `third_party/K4os.Compression.LZ4` (vendored LZ4 codec, its own assembly, pointer code throughout): not rewritten.
  Guard pages cover its allocations; its block/frame bounds are the codec's own.
- Code generated by Plank.SourceGen into *consumer* assemblies (row types in Plank.Fuzzing): `Unsafe.Add(ref
  GetArrayDataReference(slot._columnN), index)` over row-API slot arrays. Not rewritten (it is not library source);
  agent guard's `-p:PlankGuardArrays=true` page-guards those arrays (catches #276). The library side of the row API
  (`RowReaderCore.GetCurrent`) is checked (above).
- Provenance limits (README): accesses whose base cannot be attributed are counted as "unknown"
  (`PLANK_CHECKED_STATS=1`); on the committed seeds about 1-2 % of checks.
